=== LAND RAIDER PATTERN STRUCTURE ===
ENTRY hs-land-raider | Legion Land Raider Battle Squadron
  GROUP hs-lr-patterns | Land Raider Patterns | min=1 field=selections scope=parent; max=3 field=selections scope=parent
    ENTRY hs-lr-phobos | Land Raider Phobos | cost=250 | max=3 field=selections scope=parent
    ENTRY hs-lr-proteus | Land Raider Proteus | cost=200 | max=3 field=selections scope=parent
    ENTRY hs-lr-achilles | Land Raider Achilles | cost=275 | max=1 field=selections scope=parent
  LR ENTRY hs-lr-phobos | Land Raider Phobos | cost=250 | max=3 field=selections scope=parent
    RULE Standard Wargear: Two Twin-linked Lascannons, Twin-linked Heavy Bolter, Searchlight and Smoke Launchers. Power of the Machine Spirit; Assault Vehicle; Transport Capacity 10.
  LR ENTRY hs-lr-proteus | Land Raider Proteus | cost=200 | max=3 field=selections scope=parent
    RULE Standard Wargear: Two Twin-linked Lascannons, Searchlight and Smoke Launchers. Power of the Machine Spirit; Transport Capacity 10.
    RULE Explorator Augury Web: The Proteus gains Scout. At the start of the controlling player’s turn before Reserve rolls, choose Disruption Mode (opponent suffers -1 to all Reserve rolls) or Relay Mode (controlling player may re-roll failed Reserve rolls) until the next controlling turn. Multiple webs give no additional benefit and only one mode may be active for the army. A Proteus with the Web has Transport Capacity 8.
  LR ENTRY hs-lr-achilles | Land Raider Achilles | cost=275 | max=1 field=selections scope=parent
    RULE Standard Wargear: Quad Launcher with Frag and Shatter Shells; two Twin-linked Multi-Meltas; Extra Armour; Armoured Ceramite; Searchlight and Smoke Launchers. Power of the Machine Spirit; Transport Capacity 6.
